Pawnbroker
Banking
Lends against pledged goods at high interest and asks no questions. The only credit open to labourers, journeymen, and anyone the banks turn away.
Backs
Merchant Guilds · Thieves' Guild
Services
Appraisal
: Informal valuation before selling elsewhere.
Goods purchase
: Buy almost anything. Price is low.
Loans (secured)
: Short-term loans against pledged goods. High interest.
Inns and taverns (district)
Trade
Lodging
Several inn districts, given over to merchants, travellers, and those who stay long.
Backs
Merchant Guilds
Services
Full accommodation
: All price points and quality levels represented across the district.
Entertainment
: Performers, games, and live music at multiple venues nightly.
Coaching inn
Transport
Lodging
A house made for travellers by coach: fresh horses stabled, departures timed, fixed mealtimes, and a passengers' waiting room. The teams are swapped out, not just given a rest.
Backs
Merchant Guilds
Services
Lodging
: Beds, meals, and stabling for travelers on the road. Priced by quality.
Horse change
: Fresh relay horses for coaches and dispatch riders. Reduces travel time significantly.
Stable district
Trade
Concentrated stabling, horse trading, and farriery. Serves merchants, military, and travelers needing fresh mounts.
Backs
Merchant Guilds
Services
Horse hire
: Rent horses for single journeys or extended travel. Quality varies by price.
Beast trainers
Horses, dogs, and falcons, each trained for its work.
Backs
Merchant Guilds
Services
Exotic animal sales
: Unusual animals sourced from distant regions. Status symbols and curiosities.
Veterinary care
: Treatment of animal illness and injury.
Animal training
: Train animals for specific purposes: guard, hunt, war, labour, entertainment.
Post relay station
Trade
A relay of fresh horses stabled at the coaching inn carries letters and small packages far faster than foot traffic could.
Backs
City-State Council
Services
Message relay
: Pass dispatches and letters along the relay chain. Fresh horses at each station.
Parcel forwarding
: Small parcels transported between relay points. Slower than messages, cheaper than couriers.
Toll bridge
Trade
A bridge with a toll house. The keeper collects passage fees and performs basic maintenance. Minor but steady revenue.
Backs
City-State Council
Services
Crossing fee
: Collect toll from all who cross. Rate varies by load and cargo.
Customs house
Law Enforcement
Duties are taken here on goods passing along the main roads. Royal or municipal officers run it.
Backs
City-State Council
Services
Cargo inspection
: Check manifests against actual goods. Flag contraband and undervalued shipments.
Duties and tariffs
: Assessment and collection of import and export duties on all goods.

Food
State-administered granary network holding strategic reserves.

Services
Strategic grain reserves
: City-scale grain storage for siege survival and famine prevention.
Warehouse district
Warehouse
Trade
Storage set aside for the merchants' stock.

Merchant Guilds
Services
Goods storage
: Secure, dry storage for large quantities of trade goods.
Public bathhouse
Trade
Sanitation
A public bathhouse with hot water, a gathering place in some cultures and a suspected den of vice in others. Nowhere in the district hears more news.
Backs
City-State Council
Services
Bathing
: Hot and cold baths available for a small fee. Essential in cities.
Auction house
Market
Formal venue for selling high-value goods: estates, ships, livestock, art, and the contents of great houses. Charges buyer's and seller's premiums.
Backs
Merchant Guilds
Services
Estate sales
: Liquidate the possessions of a deceased or bankrupt party.
Public auction
: Open competitive bidding for goods, property, livestock, and estates.
Mint (official)
Banking
Coin struck under state or noble charter. It sets a single standard of currency across the region and returns a steady revenue through seigniorage.
Backs
City-State Council
Services
Bullion exchange
: Convert raw silver and gold into official coin at the assayed rate.
Coin production
: Strike official coinage from bullion. Quality and weight guaranteed by authority.
Chroniclers' exchange
Legal
Information
Brokerage
A guild house for news: paid correspondents on several roads, an archive that cross-checks one road against another, and a standing rate for a written answer. Expensive to keep, and the only counter in the city where a claim is graded before it is sold.
Backs
Merchant Guilds
Services
Archive consultation
: Search the archive that cross-checks one road's report against another's.
Claim grading
: Have a claim graded against every road the exchange keeps.
Written answer
: Commission a written answer on a named subject, graded and sealed.

: The complete range of arcane services from multiple practitioners.

Education
A formal college of magic. A complete curriculum, rooms for research, and a stream of visiting adepts.

Services
Arcane instruction
: Formal magical education. Foundational theory, component handling, safe casting.

Arcane
Enchanting
Magic items made to order.
Backs

Services
Enchantment services
: Apply magical properties to weapons, armour, tools, and objects to commission.
Item identification

Scroll scribe
Arcane
Scrolls of spells across the counter: the smallest charm at a craftsman's month of pay, a greater working at a merchant's year.
Backs
Arcane Orders
Services
Document writing
: Write contracts, letters, and official documents for illiterate clients.
Letter drafting
: Compose letters on behalf of clients. Discretion assured.
Legal documents
: Properly formatted wills, deeds, and binding contracts.
Crafts
29 institutions
Mills
Essential
Wheels turn on every usable stream, many licensed by guild or city.
Backs
City-State Council
Services
Grain grinding
: Grind grain into flour for bread and other foods.
Blacksmiths
Metalwork
Farriers, toolsmiths, and general forges in every quarter.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Repairs
: Reforge broken tools, re-edge blades, mend ironwork. Often more valuable than new.
Tool manufacture
: Axes, ploughs, hinges, nails. The foundation of agricultural and construction work.
Weapon smithing
: Swords, spearheads, daggers. Military-grade work requires a skilled smith.

Luxury
Swordsmiths, armourers, jewellers. The trades kept to separate guilds.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Precision metalwork
: Complex metal components for clocks, mechanisms, and specialist tools.

Luxury
Trade
The trade in silk, spice, and precious things.

Merchant Guilds
Services
Custom commissions
: Bespoke luxury items made to the client's specification.
High-end retail
: Jewellery, fine clothing, and luxury goods from across the world.
Carpenters
Guild-organised carpenters, joiners, and furniture makers for building and fitting out.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Furniture making
: Tables, chairs, chests, beds, and cabinetry for household and commercial use.
Cooper
Builds barrels for storing and hauling goods.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Barrel making
: Watertight barrels for ale, wine, salted meat, and fish. Essential packaging for trade.
Cask repair
: Re-coopering and repair of used barrels. Cheaper than new for small leaks.
Apothecary district
Healing
Trade
Multiple apothecary shops clustered together. Competition drives specialization. Some focus on chirurgery, others on herbal preparations, others on imported medicines. Herbalism kits, medicinal herbs, surgical supplies, and basic antidotes all available.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Herbal remedies
: Wholesale and retail herbs, tinctures, and prepared medicines.
Specialist compounds
: Rare medications and treatments available through district-wide sourcing.
Bowyers & fletchers (guild)
Military
Trade
Guild bowyers and fletchers: standard arrows by the sheaf, specialty broadheads, composite bows to commission. Supplies the watch, the garrison, and the caravans.
Backs
Craft Guilds · War Council
Services
Arrow production
: Consistent, high-volume arrow production for military and hunting.
Military supply
: Contract supply of bows and arrows to the local guard. Volume pricing.
Sawmill
Trade
Timber
A water- or ox-powered mill producing planks and beams for the construction trade. Employs a permanent crew.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Lumber milling
: Raw logs into planks and beams. Essential input for all construction.
Brewery
Trade
Commercial-scale ale and beer production supplying taverns and regional distribution. Major grain consumer and employer.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Ale production
: Bulk ale for taverns and households. Staple beverage where water is unsafe.
Vintner
Food
Trade
Wine pressed and matured from local fruit or imported must, when either comes to the cellar, and sold in bulk or direct to wealthy houses.
Backs
Merchant Guilds
Services
Table wine
: House wine by the jug or bottle.
Wine wholesale
: Supply to taverns and inns. Contract pricing.
Tanners
Leather
The making of leather. Sited downwind and downstream of the rest where a metropolis can manage it.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Hide processing
: Basic tanning of animal hides into usable leather.
Fuller
Trade
Finishes woven cloth by cleaning and thickening it. Often water-powered. Transforms raw weave into durable textile.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Fulled cloth
: Washed and thickened cloth. Felted and durable.
Dyer
Trade
Colours cloth, whether raw fibre or finished weave. Without it there is nothing but undyed grey wool.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Dyed cloth
: Coloured fabric. Madder red cheapest, indigo expensive.
Weavers/Textile workers
Textile
Cloth-work, more often than not guild-run.
Backs
Craft Guilds · Merchant Guilds
Services
Cloth production
: Weave wool, linen, and other fibres into cloth for clothing and trade.
Potter
Trade
A wheel and a small kiln for domestic pottery: plates, jugs, storage vessels, made whenever clay can be had.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Fired brick
: Kiln-fired clay bricks for construction.
Pottery and ceramics
: Plates, jugs, storage crocks. Functional and cheap.
Brickmaker
Trade
Moulds and a kiln for building brick, fired whenever clay can be had; brick allows sturdier, more lasting construction. The work depends on clay and fuel.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Roof tiles
: Curved and flat clay tiles.

Organised cobblers who shoe the metropolis in boots, shoes, and sandals, with quality kept under strict regulation.

Meat markets and shambles kept under strict civic rules.

Bakehouses throughout the wards, the loaf's weight and price fixed by guild rule.

Craft Guilds
Services
Fresh bread

Trade

Craft Guilds
Services
Iron refining
: Smelt raw ore into pig iron and refined ingots ready for smithing.
Chandler
Trade
Makes candles of tallow and beeswax, and turns like materials to soap and rope. The metropolis depends on it for lighting.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Beeswax candles
: Premium candles. Cleaner burn, better light.
Soap
: Lye and tallow soap.
Tallow candles
: Standard household candles.
Glassmakers
Luxury
Windows, vessels, mirrors. Requires expertise.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Glassware
: Cups, bottles, vials, and tableware. Practical and decorative.
Window glass
: Flat glass for windows. A sign of prosperity in any building.
Ropemaker
Trade
Rope and cordage twisted from hemp, flax, or whatever plant fibre comes to hand. Nothing sails, rises, or is harvested without it.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Rope (standard)
: Hemp rope in coils. Standard gauges stocked.
Midwife
Healing
Attends births, handles the hard labours, and gives what women's care there is. No medical service in a settlement is called upon more.
Backs
Religious Authorities
Services
Basic wound care
: Clean wounds, set minor fractures, reduce fever.
Birth assistance
: Experienced midwifery. Available day and night.
Woodcarver
Trade
Religious
Carves both plain and decorative pieces from wood: handles for tools, religious figures, furniture inlay. Sacred images and reliquaries are carved here too.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Carved goods
: Spoons, bowls, handles, toys. Functional items.
Religious carvings
: Devotional figures, holy images, and reliquaries.
Cartographer's guild
Education
Trade
Professional cartographers, guild-organised, producing regional maps, property surveys, and reconnaissance maps for the army. Some sell off the shelf, others are drawn to order. The city's are the most accurate maps the region has.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Commissioned maps
: Accurate maps of regions, trade routes, coastlines, and interiors.
Survey expeditions
: Send guild surveyors to map unknown or poorly-documented regions.
Craft guilds
Guild
Over a hundred distinct craft specializations. A guild for every conceivable trade.
Backs
Craft Guilds
Services
Major contracts
: Only guild members can bid on large civic and military supply contracts.
Market monopoly enforcement
: Maintain exclusive production rights for guild trades. Non-members prosecuted.
Standards body
: Set and enforce quality standards across all guild crafts.

Workhouse
Civic
Trade
The able-bodied poor receive shelter and food in exchange for compulsory labour: textile work, grinding, construction. Not a prison but the line is blurry. Reduces vagrancy and produces goods. Conditions are deliberately harsh to discourage dependency.

## Economics & Trade
Economics & Trade
Poor. Its people make their living from iron ore; it grows less food than it eats, and imports make up nearly all of the gap.
Prosperity
Poor
Complexity
Diversified — broad institutional economic base
Trade
Road
Prosperity: Moderate before the war; Poor while it lasts.
It gets about two thirds of what its work could yield.
INCOME SOURCES
Criminal Syndicate Revenue
33%
Guild-run fencing, extortion and black-market trade bring money in here, but it goes to the criminals who run them and the treasury sees none of it.
Market Taxes
16%
District-level duties on specialized goods; a major civic revenue stream across the urban districts.
Guild Licensing
11%
Charter fees, quality inspection levies, and licensing of all trades and crafts.
Financial Services
8%
Civic taxes on banking operations, letters of credit, and financial transaction fees.
Church Tithes
7%
Tithes, offerings, and fees for burial and sacramental services collected by resident clergy.
Property Rents
7%
Ground rents on civic-owned buildings, stalls, and residential plots within the walls.
Court Fees & Fines
4%
Filing fees, fines levied on offenders, and fees for notarial and legal certification services.
Pilgrim Trade
4%
Offerings, hospitality fees, relic sales, and incidental commerce from visiting pilgrims.
Iron & Metalwork
3%
Local ore feeds the smithy directly. Metalwork income is not trade-route dependent.
Wool & Textile Trade
3%
Cloth is woven or finished here.
Apothecary & Alchemy
2%
Remedies and simple preparations, made and sold here: a modest trade.
Spellcasting Services
2%
Fees for identification, minor enchanting, and divination. Adventurers and merchants both pay well for reliable magical services.

## Power Structure
Power Structure
City-State Council holds the seat, but more weight lies with Religious Authorities.
PUBLIC LEGITIMACY
## 28
28
Legitimacy Crisis
Governance fractured.
STABILITY
Tense (requisition and conscription)
GOVERNING
City-State Council
Government
GOVERNANCE
The City-State Council governs Pianomare. Its seat is held by Regulus Vetus, the exchange master and First Councillor. Insecurity and poverty have cost the City-State Council its standing. Few in Pianomare still accept its right to rule. The Religious Authorities are at odds with it. Nothing large is settled in the hall without bargaining with them first. Conscription has taken the workers, and every household is short-handed. The army's supply carts take what they need first, and local needs come second. Prices have risen and will rise again. An army quartermaster came last week and left with a list of what will be taken next month, and those in charge signed it because they saw no other choice.
POWER DISTRIBUTION
Religious Authorities 26
City-State Council 18
War Council 13
Thieves' Guild 13
Merchant Guilds 8
Arcane Orders 7
The Underground 7
Craft Guilds 5
The Trade Compact 3
POWERS
Religious Authorities
Religious
Power 26
Strong
Church holds substantial temporal power; tithes fund civic works and the clergy's opinion on appointments carries decisive weight. The pastoral burden of wartime (soldiers praying before departure, families grieving) has made the institution indispensable in a way it wasn't before. The arcane orders are present but operate in the clergy's shadow. Questions of what is sanctioned magic and what is heresy remain deliberately unresolved. They denounce the guild from the pulpit; the guild gives openly to the poor and makes the denunciations look selective.
City-State Council
Government
Governing
Power 18
Significant
A city-state council governs with considerable autonomy; camps within it compete for control of policy.
War Council
Military
Power 13
Minor
Emergency body with powers over requisition, conscription, and price controls. Unpopular. Accused of favouritism in contract awards. Probably correct on the military decisions. They are always at the table, but cannot set their own terms.
Thieves' Guild
Criminal
Power 13
Minor
Organised criminal network controls the black market and several informal revenue streams; present in council discussions through intermediaries. Shortage and requisition have pushed trade into the black market, where the guild takes its cut.
Merchant Guilds
Economy
Power 8
Suppressed
Merchants with local reach; they trade here, but have little say in how the place is run. Trade disruption and requisition are hurting the bottom line. The power is lobbying for compensation and receiving promises. The craft guilds are present but outpaced; merchants set prices, craft masters object, and merchants win more often than not. The criminal network reaches further than the merchant guilds right now. Some merchants are paying for protection; others have become silent partners.
Arcane Orders
Magic
Power 7
Suppressed
Magical practitioners are consulted but not formally empowered. Their influence is advisory, transactional, and quietly resented. The clergy have more say in public affairs, so the arcane orders work around them and never challenge them openly.
The Underground
Criminal
Power 7
Minor
A rival criminal network that wants a share of the black market and its takings, and contests them with the established one.
Craft Guilds
Economy
Power 5
Suppressed
The trades regulate their own production and apprenticeships, but carry little weight beyond the workshops. Trade disruption and requisition are hurting the bottom line. The power is lobbying for compensation and receiving promises. The merchants consistently outvote them on pricing and labour standards, so craft masters now win what they can by adding their own conditions to each deal.
The Trade Compact
Economy
Power 3
Suppressed
The trades regulate their own production and apprenticeships, but carry little weight beyond the workshops. Trade disruption and requisition are hurting the bottom line. The power is lobbying for compensation and receiving promises.

THE HALL
Living with the criminals. Nobody at the head of the City-State Council answers to the Thieves' Guild, and the City-State Council has not set out to stamp the Thieves' Guild out.

## Defense & Security
Defense & Security
Well-defended: massive walls and fortifications, held by the multiple garrisons. It leans on magic to hold.
ACTIVE MILITARY STATUS
Wartime
In wartime Pianomare watches its roads for deserters, spies and the army's own requisition officers alike.
Readiness
Well-defended
Safety
Tense — wartime
Watch to population
0.1
Food resilience
68
GUARD ASSESSMENT
The garrison and city watch maintain standard patrol coverage. Effective against opportunistic crime; less effective against organized operations that can plan around patrol routes. Thieves here know that the court sits and the cells are real. The garrison and city watch keep the gates, but smugglers have ways in beneath the walls. Hired swords back the garrison when the purse allows.
THREAT ASSESSMENT
Each bar shows how ready the settlement is for that threat, as judged at the first survey; Disasters & Famine is re-judged as your campaign goes on. A longer bar is safer.
Beasts & Monsters
Strong
Active frontier. Walls and garrison provide credible deterrence: most creature threats will not press a defended perimeter. Charter hall handles anything above the garrison usual remit. Adequate for the threat level.
Invasion & War
Strong
Walls and professional garrison provide meaningful deterrence against raiding and conventional assault. Not rated for sustained siege without significant supply stockpiles.
Internal Security
Adequate
Internal security: Tense — Wartime. Arrest, trial and gaol all work here.
Economic Survival
Adequate
Money enough for a short crisis, laid by before the war; a long one would start eating the reserves within months.
Disasters & Famine
Strong
The hospital can isolate the sick and keep an outbreak from spreading.
Food endurance :
Under siege the outlying fields would be lost; the granary would carry the metropolis for more than a year at full rations, more than two years on half, and what is still harvested would stretch it further.
ARMED FORCES & FORTIFICATIONS
Fortifications
Massive walls and fortifications
A defence built in layers, an outer wall and an inner wall, with gatehouses throughout.
Citadel
The inner fortress, and the last refuge when a siege closes in.
Standing forces
Multiple garrisons
Garrison forces distributed across quarters. No single barracks can secure a metropolis.
Professional city watch
A standing force of the law, roughly one in a hundred residents.
Contracted forces
Mercenary quarter
Sellsword companies under proper organisation, mustering hundreds to thousands.
Monster response (charter)
Multiple adventurers' guilds
Rival organisations.
Arcane defense
Mages' district
A whole quarter of arcane practitioners, all towers, workshops, libraries, and reagent sellers.
Academy of magic
A formal college of magic. A complete curriculum, rooms for research, and a stream of visiting adepts.
Enchanter's shop
Magic items made to order.
Scroll scribe
Scrolls of spells across the counter: the smallest charm at a craftsman's month of pay, a greater working at a merchant's year.
CRIMINAL ARCHITECTURE
Criminal structure · Semi-organized networks
Criminal activity is coordinated enough to maintain routes and territories but lacks a single controlling authority. Several gangs may be competing. Less predictable than a guild, more structured than street crime.
Magical crime
Smuggling
war profiteering
deserter networks

ACTIVE CRIMINAL OPERATIONS
Smuggling Network
Moves goods around customs and guild charters. Corrupt officials, unofficial landing points, and false manifests.
Rival Gangs
Controls specific territory through violence. Extorts local businesses. Competes with the law for street-level authority.
Front Businesses
Legitimate-looking operations used to launder criminal revenue and provide cover for illegal activities.
Underground City
An entire secondary economy operating below street level. Beyond enforcement reach without extraordinary effort.
Black Market Bazaar
Operates a parallel marketplace for contraband, stolen goods, and unlicensed services. Competes directly with legitimate merchants.
Assassins' Guild
Professional killers for hire. That there is work for them says how politics is done here.
Gambling District
Operates unlicensed gambling. Revenue funds broader criminal network. Attracts debt spirals and desperation crime.
Red Light District
A quarter given over to pleasure: a trade that runs at the edge of the law.
Underworld: Thieves' Guild · Minor

## Notable NPCs
Notable NPCs
88 figures
Ask who matters here and you hear 88 names; Regulus Vetus, the exchange master, First Councillor, first of City-State Council, comes first.
MAJOR FIGURES
Regulus Vetus
the exchange master
City-State Council
PWR 10
Sex
Male
·
Age
mid-fifties
·
Standing
first of City-State Council, the second strongest power here; first of The Trade Compact, the weakest power here
International trade center · First Councillor, first of City-State Council · first of The Trade Compact
Personality
scholarly
Motivation
Stop the Thieves' Guild from taking anything more from the City-State Council this season. — See the international trade center out of debt for good, with money set aside for bad years.
How they deal
Friendly and quick; by the end of the talk they know more about you than you do about them.
SECRETS
·
Secretly receives what they believe are genuine divine visions, which terrifies them: Acting on the visions would be seen as madness; ignoring them may be worse
RELATIONSHIPS
· Blanda Zoilus - Mentor and Successor
· Cassia Gratus - called by · Mentor and Successor
· Egnatius Cato - called by · Outstanding Debt
· Incitatus Balbus - called by · Outstanding Debt
Julia Publicola
the senior craft-guild master
Craft Guilds
PWR 10
Sex
Female
·
Age
late fifties
·
Standing
first of Craft Guilds, one of the lesser powers here
Craft guilds · first of Craft Guilds
Personality
stalwart
Motivation
Find out whether Felicia Salinator has been meeting anyone from the Craft Guilds in private. — Be the one person in the Craft Guilds that the Merchant Guilds will always agree to hear.
How they deal
Helpful within the limits of their role. Refers you to others for anything outside it.
SECRETS
·
Has been funding a forbidden relationship with embezzled money: Both the relationship and the embezzlement would become public simultaneously
RELATIONSHIPS
· Felix Eques - Mentor and Successor
· Aurelia Imperiosus - called by · Mentor and Successor
Sabina Volso
the mine captain
City-State Council
PWR 10
Sex
Female
·
Age
mid-fifties
Mine · member of City-State Council · member of Merchant Guilds
Personality
Flaw: reckless
Motivation
Get the most out of the work at the mine while it runs well, and put the gains by. — Win rights for every house that does the same work as the mine, not for the mine alone.
How they deal
Sells ore by the cart, from what the workings still give; hires workers by the week.
SECRETS
·
Covered up a cave-in that killed three men by sealing the gallery and reporting them as run off: One of the men's brothers works in the mine, and has been asking to reopen the gallery
RELATIONSHIPS
· Cornelia Gratus - Outstanding Debt
· Vipsanius Orestes - called by · Outstanding Debt
Plotina Serranus
the garrison commander
War Council
PWR 9
Sex
Female
·
Age
late forties
·
Standing
first of War Council, the joint third strongest power here
Multiple garrisons · first of War Council
Personality
plain-dealing · Flaw: envious
Motivation
Get one person close to Gnaeus Pansa to talk, quietly. — Bring the Thieves' Guild under the sway of the War Council, one member at a time.
How they deal
Promises help easily, and is hard to find when the time comes to give it.
SECRETS
·
Authorised the seizure of a rival's property as a 'war measure', on evidence they invented: The rival's heir survived the war the seizure was meant to fund, and has come home with the original documents
RELATIONSHIPS
· Corvus Plancus - Mentor and Successor
· Gaius Pulvillus - called by · Outstanding Debt
· Valerianus Flavius - called by · Outstanding Debt

Standing
first of Merchant Guilds, one of the lesser powers here
Merchant guilds · first of Merchant Guilds
Personality
Flaw: cruel
Motivation
Keep Falco Metellus in second place by giving them enough to want and nothing to take. — See the Craft Guilds too weak to threaten the Merchant Guilds again.
How they deal
Pleasant until crossed; then the warmth goes at once and does not come back.
SECRETS
·
Owes Felix Eques a debt from before either of them held their current position: one that Felix Eques has never formally called in: The silence feels like patience, and it is not forgiveness
RELATIONSHIPS
· Cornelia Gratus - Mentor and Successor

Age

Standing
first of The Underground, one of the lesser powers here
Underground city · first of The Underground
Personality
discerning · Flaw: arrogant
Motivation
Keep Varus Calvus busy with real work for the Underground, too busy to gather friends of their own. — Use what Felix Eques owes to bind them as an ally for life.
How they deal
Gracious in the way of someone doing you a kindness, and expects to be thanked for it.
SECRETS
·
Their legitimate business is a front for moving contraband: Seizure of everything; the legitimacy is thin and investigators are circling
RELATIONSHIPS

Mages' district · first of Arcane Orders
Personality
protective · Flaw: overbearing
Motivation
Win the mages' district first call on any help those who govern can give this season. — Never end up in Piso Gurges's shadow.
How they deal
Gracious in the way of someone doing you a kindness, and expects to be thanked for it.
SECRETS
·
Made a binding pact with an entity they now cannot name without consequence: The pact will come due (soon), and the terms are worse than they appeared at signing

Merchant Guilds
PWR 8
Sex
Male
·
Age
late sixties
Mercenary quarter · third of Merchant Guilds · member of War Council
Personality
protective · Flaw: callous
Motivation
Do Blanda Zoilus the next favour quickly, and ask for more coin in return. — Rise past Falco Metellus in the Merchant Guilds on their own merit, owing no one for the climb.
How they deal
Treats every question as a possible threat, and remembers who asked it.
SECRETS
·
Knows the location of a sealed magical site that others have been searching for: Everyone who wants it would kill for the information; none of them should have it
RELATIONSHIPS
· Blanda Zoilus - Silent Partnership

Motive:
Win one ally of the City-State Council over to the side of the Religious Authorities. — Secure a steady supply of stone for the great cathedral that no one else can cut off.

the luxury merchant
Merchant Guilds
Motive:
Keep the luxury goods quarter working through the season even if the supply of luxury materials should fail. — Pass on to someone new what passed between them and Vipsanius Orestes.
Lucius Cordus

the chancellor
Arcane Orders
Motive:
Keep the accounts of the academy of magic balanced, and show them to anyone who asks. — Never again be told what to do.
Junia Regulus
PWR 4
the dean
Religious Authorities
Motive:
Learn what Pompeia Fronto has been saying about them, and to whom. — End the bad blood between the Religious Authorities and the City-State Council before the next generation inherits it.

## Relationships
Relationships
PROMINENT RELATIONSHIP
Aemilius Afer & Blanda Zoilus · Unresolved History
Internal relationships
•
Plotina Serranus and Corvus Plancus · Mentor and Successor
Corvus Plancus still uses Plotina Serranus's methods and her ledger format. Everyone who sees Corvus Plancus's work recognises the teacher.
Plotina Serranus has asked Corvus Plancus for a loan. It is the first time a debt between teacher and student has run that way.
•
Junia Regulus and Pompeia Fronto · Mutual Leverage
Junia Regulus and Pompeia Fronto deal with each other slowly and politely, each knowing one slip could ruin them both.
Pompeia Fronto has recently secured something that makes Junia Regulus's hold obsolete. The politeness continues, for now.
•
Horatius Maximus and Cornelia Gratus · Mentor and Successor
Horatius Maximus named Cornelia Gratus as successor in front of witnesses. The formal handover has not happened.
Cornelia Gratus has begun teaching Horatius Maximus's methods as her own.
•
Regulus Vetus and Blanda Zoilus · Mentor and Successor
Regulus Vetus made Blanda Zoilus's career, and she rarely says so.
Blanda Zoilus now operates in ways Regulus Vetus would not approve of. Neither discusses the divergence directly.
•
Julia Publicola and Felix Eques · Mentor and Successor
Felix Eques owes their place to Julia Publicola and has kept others from climbing the same way. The two of them remember it differently.
Julia Publicola has one favour left to call in, and is saving it. Felix Eques knows, and is watching for it.
•
Drusilla Geta and Messalina Gurges · Mentor and Successor
Drusilla Geta taught Messalina Gurges everything, including how to get the better of the network's head.
A third party has asked Drusilla Geta, quietly, whether Messalina Gurges can be trusted. The answer took too long.
•
Geminus Caesar and Piso Gurges · Mentor and Successor
Geminus Caesar took Piso Gurges under their wing early and taught them the customs, the clients, and the names worth knowing.
Piso Gurges has taken on a client Geminus Caesar turned away on principle.
•
Helvia Scipio and Torquatus Quirinus · Mentor and Successor
Torquatus Quirinus spent seven years at Helvia Scipio's side. Half of the people Torquatus Quirinus deals with first came through Helvia Scipio's door.
Helvia Scipio has taken a new apprentice, and Torquatus Quirinus has heard the apprentice is being taught things him never was.
•
Postumia Albinus and Aemilius Afer · Professional Rivalry
Postumia Albinus and Aemilius Afer trained together and have competed ever since. The settlement keeps score, and so do they.
A post opens at midsummer, and only Postumia Albinus or Aemilius Afer can fill it.
•
Varro Barba and Quartica Nero · Silent Partnership
Varro Barba does what Quartica Nero asks, and she pays for it in coin that never passes through a counting-house. In company the two of them speak only of ordinary business.
Varro Barba has started spending Quartica Nero's coin where people can see it: a new roof, a new horse. Quartica Nero has noticed, and so have the neighbours.
•
Aemilius Afer and Blanda Zoilus · Unresolved History
Aemilius Afer and Blanda Zoilus were partners until one of them emptied the shared account. Each says it was the other.
Aemilius Afer and Blanda Zoilus have been handed the same dispute to settle together. The first meeting is next week.
•
Octavius Rufus and Blanda Zoilus · Silent Partnership
Blanda Zoilus has put money into Octavius Rufus's affairs for years and takes a share of everything him earns. No contract exists, and neither of them wants one.
Blanda Zoilus wants a larger share this year. Octavius Rufus thinks the share is large enough already and has not yet found a way to tell Blanda Zoilus so.
•
Laelia Brutus and Lavinia Varus · Genuine Respect
Laelia Brutus and Lavinia Varus disagree often and trust completely. Each has watched the other be right at cost, and neither forgets it.
Laelia Brutus is being pressed to act against Lavinia Varus, and has not mentioned it.
•
Vipsanius Orestes and Sabina Volso · Outstanding Debt
Vipsanius Orestes owes Sabina Volso a great deal, and neither has ever said so aloud.
Sabina Volso is about to call it in.
•
Quartica Nero and Postumia Albinus · Outstanding Debt
Years ago Postumia Albinus did Quartica Nero a favour too large to repay, and the whisper broker has been paying it back in small ways ever since.
Quartica Nero has begun to act like someone who considers the debt paid. Postumia Albinus does not.
•
Blanda Zoilus and Salinator Regulus · Pragmatic Alliance
Blanda Zoilus and Salinator Regulus back each other in public and check each other's work in private.
Blanda Zoilus wants to bring a third member into the alliance. Salinator Regulus thinks the newcomer would take his place.
•
Corvus Plancus and Messalina Gurges · Genuine Respect
There is no arrangement between Corvus Plancus and Messalina Gurges, only a long record of kept words; in this settlement that makes them remarkable.
Corvus Plancus and Messalina Gurges still respect each other, but what each of them wants is pulling apart, and both can see it.
•
Falco Metellus and Cicero Varro · Unresolved History
Falco Metellus blames Cicero Varro for a death in the impresario's family. Cicero Varro has never denied it.
Cicero Varro's child has asked to apprentice with Falco Metellus. Falco Metellus has not refused.
•
Plinius Pulvillus and Regulus Mamercus · Pragmatic Alliance
Plinius Pulvillus brings the votes and Regulus Mamercus brings the money. The alliance is three years old and has won every vote it needed.
Regulus Mamercus voted against Plinius Pulvillus on a small matter last week. Plinius Pulvillus is waiting to see whether it was small.
•
Pulvillus Scaeva and Varus Calvus · Silent Partnership
Pulvillus Scaeva wanted more than honest work could pay. Varus Calvus made up the difference, and now Pulvillus Scaeva passes on whatever the gang boss needs to know.
Varus Calvus has asked Pulvillus Scaeva for something the enchanter cannot give without being noticed. Pulvillus Scaeva asked for time, and Varus Calvus gave a week.
•
Varus Calvus and Pompeia Fronto · Genuine Respect
Varus Calvus sends difficult cases to Pompeia Fronto, and she sends them back solved. Neither has ever charged the other a fee.
Varus Calvus has learned something about Pompeia Fronto's family that she does not know.
•
Lavinia Varus and Cicero Vetus · Professional Rivalry
Lavinia Varus wants Cicero Vetus's position, and he wants her reputation. They are civil in public and unforgiving in private.
Cicero Vetus has been telling clients that Lavinia Varus's work is slipping. Two of the clients have repeated it to Lavinia Varus.
•
Regulus Pansa and Varro Barba · Mutual Leverage
Regulus Pansa and Varro Barba trade favours on a strict count. Neither has ever been more than one favour ahead.
Regulus Pansa has asked Varro Barba for a second favour before repaying the first, which would put the tanner two favours ahead.
•
Felicia Salinator and Torquatus Volso · Outstanding Debt
Felicia Salinator owes Torquatus Volso for an old kindness. Neither mentions it, and both know exactly how much is still owed.
Someone else has learned what favour Felicia Salinator owes Torquatus Volso, and now has a hold on her too.
•
Lavinia Hispallus and Incitatus Balbus · Outstanding Debt
Incitatus Balbus paid Lavinia Hispallus's bail eleven years ago and has never mentioned it. Lavinia Hispallus mentions it to no one.
Incitatus Balbus has asked Lavinia Hispallus for a vote against her own interests. It is the first payment Incitatus Balbus has asked for.
•
Ravilla Moderatus and Nero Julianus · Mutual Leverage
Ravilla Moderatus controls a vote Nero Julianus needs, and the toll-keeper controls a supply the theatre manager needs. Their meetings are short and their bargains hold.
Nero Julianus has found a new source for what Ravilla Moderatus supplies. Ravilla Moderatus has not been told.
•
Kaninia Falco and Messalina Gurges · Outstanding Debt
Kaninia Falco owes Messalina Gurges a large sum in coin, borrowed in a bad year and repaid in small amounts ever since.
Kaninia Falco has offered to clear what is left of the debt in one payment. Messalina Gurges would rather be repaid in favours.
•
Sabina Volso and Cornelia Gratus · Outstanding Debt
Cornelia Gratus kept Sabina Volso's name out of a scandal that ruined two others. Sabina Volso has voted with Cornelia Gratus ever since.
Cornelia Gratus has mentioned the debt aloud, at a dinner, in front of Sabina Volso's partners.
•
Julia Albinus and Trebius Celer · Genuine Respect
Julia Albinus and Trebius Celer have stood on opposite sides of three disputes and praised each other after every one.
Trebius Celer has been offered Julia Albinus's post by people who want her gone.
•
Felix Eques and Helvia Scipio · Outstanding Debt
Helvia Scipio nursed Felix Eques through a winter fever when no one else would come. Felix Eques still owes the debt and still knows it.
Felix Eques has refused Helvia Scipio a small favour for the first time in years.
•
Ursus Macer and Pulvillus Scaeva · Genuine Respect
Pulvillus Scaeva once told the council that Ursus Macer was right and the enchanter was wrong. Ursus Macer has not forgotten it.
Ursus Macer has made a mistake that Pulvillus Scaeva is bound to report. Pulvillus Scaeva has not reported it.
•
Torquatus Volso and Vipsanius Orestes · Mentor and Successor
Torquatus Volso vouched for Vipsanius Orestes when nobody else would. Vipsanius Orestes's first post came from that word.
Vipsanius Orestes corrected Torquatus Volso in public last month, and was right.
•
Sempronia Vetus and Varro Barba · Pragmatic Alliance
Sempronia Vetus and Varro Barba signed a pact after their last quarrel cost them both. They keep to its terms exactly.
Sempronia Vetus and Varro Barba both want the same office, and the alliance cannot survive one of them winning it.
•
Nero Julianus and Kaninia Falco · Mentor and Successor
Nero Julianus still gives Kaninia Falco advice now and then, unasked, and she still hears out every word of it.
Kaninia Falco has stopped answering Nero Julianus's advice, though she still follows most of it.
•
Messalina Gurges and Plinius Pulvillus · Unresolved History
Messalina Gurges and Plinius Pulvillus fought a duel in their youth. Both survived, and the scar is on Plinius Pulvillus's face.
Messalina Gurges has learned that Plinius Pulvillus is in debt and could be ruined by one call on a loan.
•
Aelius Zoilus and Clodia Rutilius · Professional Rivalry
Every friend Aelius Zoilus wins is one Clodia Rutilius lost, and the reverse. The two keep a polite distance and an exact tally.
Aelius Zoilus and Clodia Rutilius will both present proposals at the next council session. Only one will be accepted.
•
Tertulla Julianus and Plinius Pulvillus · Unresolved History
Something happened between Tertulla Julianus and Plinius Pulvillus years ago that neither has forgotten. They function professionally. They do not forgive.
What first broke Tertulla Julianus and Plinius Pulvillus apart is happening again, and both of them know it.
•
Pansa Zoilus and Clodia Rutilius · Professional Rivalry
Pansa Zoilus and Clodia Rutilius are rivals for the same patron's favour. Each has spent the year trying to be first through the patron's door.
Pansa Zoilus lost the last contract to Clodia Rutilius by a single vote, and has learned who cast it.
•
Pompeia Fronto and Horatius Strabo · Professional Rivalry
Pompeia Fronto and Horatius Strabo want the same thing and compete hard for it, but neither has let it turn into hatred.
A decision coming next season will favour Pompeia Fronto or Horatius Strabo heavily, and both of them know it.
•
Albina Flaccus and Tertulla Julianus · Genuine Respect
Albina Flaccus and Tertulla Julianus consult each other before any decision that matters. The advice is honest and is always taken seriously.
The council has asked Albina Flaccus and Tertulla Julianus to audit each other's accounts. Both agreed, and neither is comfortable.
•
Laevinus Regulus and Falco Metellus · Family Complication
Laevinus Regulus and Falco Metellus are family, but in business they deal with each other as if they were not related.
The family has taken a side in a quarrel between Laevinus Regulus and Falco Metellus, and both of them think it is the wrong side.
•
Tacitus Serranus and Torquatus Volso · Mentor and Successor
Tacitus Serranus taught Torquatus Volso patience, which the castellan never managed to learn.
Torquatus Volso means to take a pupil this year and has not yet asked Tacitus Serranus's blessing.
•
Regulus Mamercus and Clodia Rutilius · Mutual Leverage
Regulus Mamercus and Clodia Rutilius each have something to trade and nothing to give away. They meet in private, bargain hard, and keep every bargain.
The vote Clodia Rutilius needs from Regulus Mamercus comes up next week, and he has named a new price.
•
Marcus Piso and Tertulla Julianus · Family Complication
Marcus Piso married into Tertulla Julianus's family. Tertulla Julianus opposed the match and still calls Marcus Piso by surname.
Marcus Piso's child wants to marry into Tertulla Julianus's household. Neither parent has given an answer.
•
Clodia Rutilius and Varro Barba · Mutual Leverage
Varro Barba needs Clodia Rutilius's seal on certain papers; she needs his silence about certain others. Neither has asked for more than the other could pay.
Clodia Rutilius's secret is about to come out by other means. Once it does, Varro Barba holds nothing over Clodia Rutilius.
•
Cassia Gratus and Regulus Vetus · Mentor and Successor
Regulus Vetus owes their place to Cassia Gratus and has kept others from climbing the same way. The two of them remember it differently.
Cassia Gratus has one favour left to call in, and is saving it. Regulus Vetus knows, and is watching for it.
•
Gaius Pulvillus and Plotina Serranus · Outstanding Debt
Gaius Pulvillus borrowed Plotina Serranus's good name to win a post. Plotina Serranus has never asked for it back.
Plotina Serranus has begun to count the debt Gaius Pulvillus owes among her assets, and has had it written down before witnesses.
•
Decima Plancus and Messalina Gurges · Professional Rivalry
Only one of Decima Plancus and Messalina Gurges can come out ahead, and both know it. They stay polite and give no ground.
A patron they share has let it be known there is room for only Decima Plancus or Messalina Gurges at the next table.
•
Cicero Vetus and Quartica Nero · Silent Partnership
Quartica Nero's silver bought the friends who raised Cicero Vetus to where he stands today. Cicero Vetus repays it in favours, quietly and on time.
Cicero Vetus hopes to rise again this season. Quartica Nero expects the favours to grow with Cicero Vetus's standing.
•
Valerianus Flavius and Plotina Serranus · Outstanding Debt
Plotina Serranus once spoke up for Valerianus Flavius when it cost her something to do it. Valerianus Flavius has never been asked for anything in return.
Plotina Serranus has asked Valerianus Flavius to speak up for someone else, as she once spoke up for him.
•
Calvus Naso and Lavinia Varus · Outstanding Debt
Lavinia Varus stood surety for Calvus Naso once, and he has kept every term since so that she would never have to pay.
Lavinia Varus's surety for Calvus Naso still stands, and she would like to be released from it.
•
Plotina Zoilus and Albina Flaccus · Outstanding Debt
Plotina Zoilus borrowed Albina Flaccus's good name to win a post. Albina Flaccus has never asked for it back.
Albina Flaccus has begun to count the debt Plotina Zoilus owes among the prison warden's assets, and has had it written down before witnesses.
•
Maesia Scaeva and Zenobia Ursus · Mutual Leverage
Maesia Scaeva and Zenobia Ursus take opposite sides of most questions and stand together on the few that touch their shared interests.
Zenobia Ursus has written down what Maesia Scaeva owes and left a sealed copy with a third party.
•
Aurelia Imperiosus and Julia Publicola · Mentor and Successor
Aurelia Imperiosus taught Julia Publicola everything, including how to get the better of the coaching-inn keeper.
A third party has asked Aurelia Imperiosus, quietly, whether Julia Publicola can be trusted. The answer took too long.
•
Tertulla Rufus and Lavinia Varus · Unresolved History
Tertulla Rufus and Lavinia Varus were close once, and whatever broke between them is the one subject neither will discuss.
Tertulla Rufus and Lavinia Varus each think the other owes the apology.
•
Horatius Strabo and Decima Plancus · Silent Partnership
Once a month Horatius Strabo and Decima Plancus meet in a back room to split what the month brought in. In the street they pass each other without a nod.
A clerk has found a payment to Horatius Strabo that nobody can explain. Decima Plancus wants to know who the clerk talks to.
•
Cornelia Gratus and Felix Eques · Outstanding Debt
Years ago Felix Eques did Cornelia Gratus a favour too large to repay, and she has been paying it back in small ways ever since.
Cornelia Gratus has begun to act like someone who considers the debt paid. Felix Eques does not.
•
Perenna Plancus and Opimius Pulvillus · Mentor and Successor
Perenna Plancus took Opimius Pulvillus under their wing early and taught them the customs, the clients, and the names worth knowing.
Opimius Pulvillus has taken on a client Perenna Plancus turned away on principle.
•
Lucius Cordus and Postumia Albinus · Professional Rivalry
Every gain Lucius Cordus makes, Postumia Albinus answers within the season, and people here have learned to expect it.
Lucius Cordus has recently broken an unwritten rule of the contest. Postumia Albinus is deciding what that permits.
•
Pompeius Aurelius and Lavinia Hispallus · Mutual Leverage
Pompeius Aurelius and Lavinia Hispallus each hold information or resources the other needs. They cooperate efficiently and neither trusts the other at all.
The balance between Pompeius Aurelius and Lavinia Hispallus has recently shifted. Lavinia Hispallus has noticed; Pompeius Aurelius has not, yet.
•
Zosimus Ravilla and Cicero Vetus · Mutual Leverage
Zosimus Ravilla knows enough to ruin Cicero Vetus, and the house boss holds enough to finish the guild's master, so the two of them work together very carefully.
A document Zosimus Ravilla and Cicero Vetus each believed only they possessed has turned up in someone else's hands.
•
Opimius Pulvillus and Varus Calvus · Mentor and Successor
Varus Calvus spent seven years at Opimius Pulvillus's side. Half of the people Varus Calvus deals with first came through Opimius Pulvillus's door.
Opimius Pulvillus has taken a new apprentice, and Varus Calvus has heard the apprentice is being taught things the gang boss never was.
•
Salinator Regulus and Varro Barba · Mutual Leverage
Salinator Regulus and Varro Barba know exactly what each would refuse the other, and so neither ever asks for it.
Varro Barba needs something this season that lies just past what Salinator Regulus has always refused.
•
Ravilla Publicola and Nero Julianus · Pragmatic Alliance
Ravilla Publicola and Nero Julianus are allies with separate interests. They meet to coordinate and leave by different doors.
Nero Julianus has asked Ravilla Publicola to back a candidate the master metalworker distrusts. Refusing would cost the alliance its majority.
•
Gnaeus Pansa and Helvia Scipio · Silent Partnership
Gnaeus Pansa opens doors for Helvia Scipio's people, and she keeps his purse and good name growing. Both of them think they have the better half of the bargain.
Gnaeus Pansa has been seen dining with one of Helvia Scipio's rivals. Helvia Scipio has not mentioned it, and Gnaeus Pansa cannot tell whether that is good or bad.
•
Laelia Volso and Varus Calvus · Genuine Respect
Laelia Volso trusts Varus Calvus's word over any written contract. Varus Calvus has never given a reason to regret it.
Varus Calvus has publicly backed a plan that Laelia Volso thinks will fail. Laelia Volso has said so once, in private.
•
Quintus Aurelius and Cicero Varro · Pragmatic Alliance
Quintus Aurelius and Cicero Varro work together because fighting each other would cost them both more, and both of them know it.
Someone else is working to pull Quintus Aurelius and Cicero Varro apart.
•
Opimius Julianus and Marcus Piso · Professional Rivalry
Opimius Julianus and Marcus Piso sit at the same tables and want the same things from them. Each counts the other's wins more carefully than their own.
Opimius Julianus has hired away Marcus Piso's best assistant. Marcus Piso has said nothing about it, to anyone.
•
Saturninus Moderatus and Ravilla Moderatus · Mutual Leverage
Saturninus Moderatus can open doors for Ravilla Moderatus that the theatre manager could never open alone, and the theatre manager does the same for the brewer in other places. Each uses the other sparingly.
Saturninus Moderatus has asked Ravilla Moderatus for an introduction the theatre manager would rather keep to themselves.
•
Torquatus Quirinus and Laelia Brutus · Mutual Leverage
Torquatus Quirinus cannot finish what they have started without Laelia Brutus's help, and she cannot finish theirs without his. Neither lets the other forget it.
Torquatus Quirinus wants the arrangement with Laelia Brutus written down and witnessed. Laelia Brutus would rather keep it a matter of word.
•
Felicia Silanus and Regulus Pansa · Unresolved History
The quarrel between Felicia Silanus and Regulus Pansa is old enough that no one else remembers the cause. They remember. They have simply stopped explaining.
A newcomer keeps trying to reconcile Felicia Silanus and Regulus Pansa, and is about to learn why nobody else does.
•
Regulus Geta and Lavinia Varus · Silent Partnership
Regulus Geta does what Lavinia Varus asks, and she pays for it in coin that never passes through a counting-house. In company the two of them speak only of ordinary business.
Regulus Geta has started spending Lavinia Varus's coin where people can see it: a new roof, a new horse. Lavinia Varus has noticed, and so have the neighbours.
•
Accia Geta and Zosimus Ravilla · Held Over Them
Zosimus Ravilla knows where Accia Geta's sister lives, how she earns her keep and what she hides. Accia Geta has never had to be told what would happen if she refused Zosimus Ravilla.
Accia Geta's sister has been seen talking to Zosimus Ravilla's people. Accia Geta does not know whether she went to them or they came to her.
•
Fabius Vetus and Tertia Gurges · Professional Rivalry
Fabius Vetus and Tertia Gurges have made each other better by the contest, and each would admit it, though never to the other.
Tertia Gurges has come out ahead three times running. Fabius Vetus has said nothing and started earlier each morning.
•
Trebius Celer and Falco Metellus · Outstanding Debt
Trebius Celer owes Falco Metellus for an old kindness. Neither mentions it, and both know exactly how much is still owed.
Someone else has learned what favour Trebius Celer owes Falco Metellus, and now has a hold on the master butcher too.
•
Tertia Gurges and Regulus Geta · Professional Rivalry
Tertia Gurges and Regulus Geta were friends before they were rivals, and still are on the days the contest rests.
Tertia Gurges needs Regulus Geta's help with something outside the contest, and has not yet found a way to ask.
•
Albinus Scaeva and Quintus Aurelius · Professional Rivalry
Every friend Albinus Scaeva wins is one Quintus Aurelius lost, and the reverse. The two keep a polite distance and an exact tally.
Albinus Scaeva and Quintus Aurelius will both present proposals at the next council session. Only one will be accepted.
•
Egnatius Cato and Regulus Vetus · Outstanding Debt
Regulus Vetus paid Egnatius Cato's bail eleven years ago and has never mentioned it. Egnatius Cato mentions it to no one.
Regulus Vetus has asked Egnatius Cato for a vote against the chandler's own interests. It is the first payment Regulus Vetus has asked for.
•
Manlia Cato and Varro Barba · Mutual Leverage
Manlia Cato needs Varro Barba's goodwill this year, and he will need her the next. Both of them have counted the years.
Manlia Cato's year of need has come early, and Varro Barba has not yet decided how generous to be.
•
Piso Gurges and Opimius Julianus · Family Complication
Piso Gurges and Opimius Julianus are cousins on opposite sides of the settlement's politics. Their grandmother still seats them together.
Opimius Julianus has gone into business against Piso Gurges's partners, and the family expects the ropemaker to keep the peace.
•
Zenobia Ursus and Varus Calvus · Held Over Them
Zenobia Ursus ran goods for Varus Calvus's crew before she was anyone here. Varus Calvus remembers every run and the name of everyone who saw it.
One of Zenobia Ursus's old crewmates has been taken by the garrison and may talk to save their own neck. Varus Calvus could get the prisoner out if Zenobia Ursus asks, and she knows what asking will cost.
•
Iulia Geta and Gaius Pulvillus · Mentor and Successor
Iulia Geta vouched for Gaius Pulvillus when nobody else would. Gaius Pulvillus's first post came from that word.
Gaius Pulvillus corrected Iulia Geta in public last month, and was right.
•
Titus Serranus and Varro Barba · Mutual Leverage
Titus Serranus hears of trouble early and Varro Barba hears of opportunity early, and each passes the word to the other a day before anyone else.
Titus Serranus learned of something last week and told Varro Barba a day late. Varro Barba has noticed the day.
•
Cicero Varro and Cassia Gratus · Held Over Them
Years ago Cicero Varro killed someone in a fight, and Cassia Gratus's people hid the body. Cicero Varro has done what Cassia Gratus asks ever since, because she knows where the body lies.
Someone has started digging near the place where the body was hidden. Cassia Gratus wants Cicero Varro to put a stop to it, and he has no reason to give that would not raise questions.
•
Nerva Opimius and Nero Julianus · Genuine Respect
Nerva Opimius and Nero Julianus have each watched the other do good work. They argue, but each trusts the other's judgment in their own field.
Nerva Opimius and Nero Julianus are about to be on opposite sides of an important decision.
•
Incitatus Balbus and Regulus Vetus · Outstanding Debt
Incitatus Balbus owes Regulus Vetus a large sum in coin, borrowed in a bad year and repaid in small amounts ever since.
Incitatus Balbus has offered to clear what is left of the debt in one payment. Regulus Vetus would rather be repaid in favours.
•
Helvia Pansa and Julia Albinus · Mutual Leverage
Helvia Pansa and Julia Albinus take opposite sides of most questions and stand together on the few that touch their shared interests.
Julia Albinus has written down what Helvia Pansa owes and left a sealed copy with a third party.
•
Pompeius Capito and Helvia Pansa · Genuine Respect
Pompeius Capito and Helvia Pansa disagree often and trust completely. Each has watched the other be right at cost, and neither forgets it.
Pompeius Capito is being pressed to act against Helvia Pansa, and has not mentioned it.

## Identity & Daily Life
Identity & Daily Life

CULTURAL NOTES
Public standing is negotiated through family networks, office, clientage, law, and visible contribution to civic life. Masonry courtyard buildings and tiled roofs gather around public squares and processional streets.
IDENTITY
Name
Pianomare
Tier
Metropolis
Population
75,429
Terrain
Riverside
Layout
River delta metropolis: island districts, bridge-markets, the great river-gate controlling all upstream trade
Age
373 years
Trade access
Road
Founded
the slow accretion of three smaller settlements that eventually merged under a single administration
CULTURAL IDENTITY
Latin-inspired
Latin-inspired. Ask about the place and you are told it is built in masonry around a civic square, and run by patronage and law.
Built form
Masonry courtyard buildings and tiled roofs gather around public squares and processional streets.
Civic pattern
Civic office and private patronage are intertwined; public generosity is a recognized route to influence.
Exchange
Contract, warehousing, tolls, and notarial practice support long-distance commerce.
Foodways
Market vendors and shared dining customs make food distribution visibly civic.
Sacred life
Shrines, processions, confraternities, and formal priesthoods share the public religious calendar.
Social texture
Public standing is negotiated through family networks, office, clientage, law, and visible contribution to civic life.
Architecture
arcaded market fronts define the principal square
DAILY LIFE
FOOD BALANCE
What is grown here and brought in falls short of what people eat each day, so some households eat less than they need.
